# `Assembly.lean` implementation audit by Chapter 1 diagram node

## Authority and scope

`to_formalize/erdos_64_proof.tex` is the sole authority for mathematical statements,
hypotheses, alternatives, order, and terminal behavior. Live Lean declaration types, bodies,
exact key lists, call sites, and build results are the authority for what the implementation
does. Lean doc comments, block comments, and `-- EG-NODE` annotations are search locators only
and are never evidence.

The two tables below are the sole implementation-status authority. Every verdict must be
supported by current declaration types, bodies, literal ExactLedger ancestry, call sites, and
build results; a green mark or a successful narrow build by itself is not evidence of manuscript
fidelity. The node table keeps the manuscript's aggregate numeric row `[172]` for the drawn
subnodes `[172a]`–`[172c]`.

## Status rubric

Three independent properties are tracked, and passing one never implies another.

**Gate A — mechanical composition.** Whether the Lean term exists, elaborates, and depends on no
unfinished producer. Measured by `#print axioms` against a tracer inserted for each undefined
frontier producer. Gate A says nothing about what the propositions mean.

**Gate B — manuscript fidelity.** Whether the proposition the row actually publishes — the
`Holds` branch of its produced `Spine.Key` — is the manuscript's statement. Because `FactSystem`
values are data-free, all mathematical content lives in these propositions. A row whose `Holds`
is weaker than the manuscript's lemma, or trivially satisfiable, composes and closes,
and Gate A cannot detect it. Gate B is established only by reading the proposition against the
manuscript.

**Gate C — framework compliance.** Whether the step uses only the canonical `ExactLedger` /
`FactManifest` / sealed `FactInputs` / `AtomicCT.run` / `Decision.run` path, states its proposition
about the literal active residual from `inputs.current`, and projects every constant from the
problem presentation.

“Local” and “Residual-local proof?” require a proposition about the literal active
minimal-counterexample residual. “Reads ledger” and “Writes ledger” require semantic-key reads
and publication through `FactManifest.Produces` or one arm of `Decision`. “Wired” and “Correctly
wired?” require the final endpoint to traverse the exact predecessor and branch. “Legal” and
“Custom illegal carrier?” enforce the proof-specific declaration boundary and the canonical APIs.

Status legend: ✅ verified from the current type, body, and build; ⚠ concrete partial
implementation or unresolved defect; ❌ absent or verified failure; N/A where the column does not
apply; — unknown, with no verdict claimed.
